self = <scippnexus.base.Group object at 0x7f7d767749e0>, sel = 'beam_monitor_m3'

    def __getitem__(self, sel):
        """
        Get a child group or child dataset, a selection of child groups, or load and
        return the current group.
    
        Three cases are supported:
    
        - String name: The child group or child dataset of that name is returned.
        - Class such as ``NXdata`` or ``NXlog``: A dict containing all direct children
          with a matching ``NX_class`` attribute are returned. Also accepts a tuple of
          classes. ``Field`` selects all child fields, i.e., all datasets but not
          groups.
        - Scipp-style index: Load the specified slice of the current group, returning
          a :class:`scipp.DataArray` or :class:`scipp.DataGroup`.
    
        Parameters
        ----------
        sel:
            Child name, class, or index.
    
        Returns
        -------
        :
            Field, group, dict of fields, or loaded data.
        """
        if isinstance(sel, str):
            # We cannot get the child directly from the HDF5 group, since we need to
            # create the parent group, to ensure that fields get the correct properties
            # such as sizes and dtype.
            if '/' in sel:
                sel_path = PurePosixPath(sel)
                if sel_path.is_absolute():
                    return self.file[sel_path.relative_to('/').as_posix()]
                # If the path is a single name, we can directly access the child
                elif len(sel_path.parts) == 1:
                    return self[sel_path.as_posix()]
                else:
                    grp = sel_path.parts[0]
                    return self[grp][sel_path.relative_to(grp).as_posix()]
            elif sel == '..':
                return self.parent
>           child = self._children[sel]
                    ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

self = <scippnexus.base.Group object at 0x7fab98181f10>, sel = 'beam_monitor_m3'

    def __getitem__(self, sel):
        """
        Get a child group or child dataset, a selection of child groups, or load and
        return the current group.
    
        Three cases are supported:
    
        - String name: The child group or child dataset of that name is returned.
        - Class such as ``NXdata`` or ``NXlog``: A dict containing all direct children
          with a matching ``NX_class`` attribute are returned. Also accepts a tuple of
          classes. ``Field`` selects all child fields, i.e., all datasets but not
          groups.
        - Scipp-style index: Load the specified slice of the current group, returning
          a :class:`scipp.DataArray` or :class:`scipp.DataGroup`.
    
        Parameters
        ----------
        sel:
            Child name, class, or index.
    
        Returns
        -------
        :
            Field, group, dict of fields, or loaded data.
        """
        if isinstance(sel, str):
            # We cannot get the child directly from the HDF5 group, since we need to
            # create the parent group, to ensure that fields get the correct properties
            # such as sizes and dtype.
            if '/' in sel:
                sel_path = PurePosixPath(sel)
                if sel_path.is_absolute():
                    return self.file[sel_path.relative_to('/').as_posix()]
                # If the path is a single name, we can directly access the child
                elif len(sel_path.parts) == 1:
                    return self[sel_path.as_posix()]
                else:
                    grp = sel_path.parts[0]
                    return self[grp][sel_path.relative_to(grp).as_posix()]
            elif sel == '..':
                return self.parent
>           child = self._children[sel]

self = <scippnexus.base.Group object at 0x7fb5fc98f8f0>, sel = 'beam_monitor_m3'

    def __getitem__(self, sel):
        """
        Get a child group or child dataset, a selection of child groups, or load and
        return the current group.
    
        Three cases are supported:
    
        - String name: The child group or child dataset of that name is returned.
        - Class such as ``NXdata`` or ``NXlog``: A dict containing all direct children
          with a matching ``NX_class`` attribute are returned. Also accepts a tuple of
          classes. ``Field`` selects all child fields, i.e., all datasets but not
          groups.
        - Scipp-style index: Load the specified slice of the current group, returning
          a :class:`scipp.DataArray` or :class:`scipp.DataGroup`.
    
        Parameters
        ----------
        sel:
            Child name, class, or index.
    
        Returns
        -------
        :
            Field, group, dict of fields, or loaded data.
        """
        if isinstance(sel, str):
            # We cannot get the child directly from the HDF5 group, since we need to
            # create the parent group, to ensure that fields get the correct properties
            # such as sizes and dtype.
            if '/' in sel:
                sel_path = PurePosixPath(sel)
                if sel_path.is_absolute():
                    return self.file[sel_path.relative_to('/').as_posix()]
                # If the path is a single name, we can directly access the child
                elif len(sel_path.parts) == 1:
                    return self[sel_path.as_posix()]
                else:
                    grp = sel_path.parts[0]
                    return self[grp][sel_path.relative_to(grp).as_posix()]
            elif sel == '..':
                return self.parent
>           child = self._children[sel]
                    ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

self = <scippnexus.base.Group object at 0x7f47da37e330>, sel = 'beam_monitor_m3'

    def __getitem__(self, sel):
        """
        Get a child group or child dataset, a selection of child groups, or load and
        return the current group.
    
        Three cases are supported:
    
        - String name: The child group or child dataset of that name is returned.
        - Class such as ``NXdata`` or ``NXlog``: A dict containing all direct children
          with a matching ``NX_class`` attribute are returned. Also accepts a tuple of
          classes. ``Field`` selects all child fields, i.e., all datasets but not
          groups.
        - Scipp-style index: Load the specified slice of the current group, returning
          a :class:`scipp.DataArray` or :class:`scipp.DataGroup`.
    
        Parameters
        ----------
        sel:
            Child name, class, or index.
    
        Returns
        -------
        :
            Field, group, dict of fields, or loaded data.
        """
        if isinstance(sel, str):
            # We cannot get the child directly from the HDF5 group, since we need to
            # create the parent group, to ensure that fields get the correct properties
            # such as sizes and dtype.
            if '/' in sel:
                sel_path = PurePosixPath(sel)
                if sel_path.is_absolute():
                    return self.file[sel_path.relative_to('/').as_posix()]
                # If the path is a single name, we can directly access the child
                elif len(sel_path.parts) == 1:
                    return self[sel_path.as_posix()]
                else:
                    grp = sel_path.parts[0]
                    return self[grp][sel_path.relative_to(grp).as_posix()]
            elif sel == '..':
                return self.parent
>           child = self._children[sel]
                    ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

self = <scippnexus.base.Group object at 0x7f5152430d10>, sel = 'beam_monitor_m3'

    def __getitem__(self, sel):
        """
        Get a child group or child dataset, a selection of child groups, or load and
        return the current group.
    
        Three cases are supported:
    
        - String name: The child group or child dataset of that name is returned.
        - Class such as ``NXdata`` or ``NXlog``: A dict containing all direct children
          with a matching ``NX_class`` attribute are returned. Also accepts a tuple of
          classes. ``Field`` selects all child fields, i.e., all datasets but not
          groups.
        - Scipp-style index: Load the specified slice of the current group, returning
          a :class:`scipp.DataArray` or :class:`scipp.DataGroup`.
    
        Parameters
        ----------
        sel:
            Child name, class, or index.
    
        Returns
        -------
        :
            Field, group, dict of fields, or loaded data.
        """
        if isinstance(sel, str):
            # We cannot get the child directly from the HDF5 group, since we need to
            # create the parent group, to ensure that fields get the correct properties
            # such as sizes and dtype.
            if '/' in sel:
                sel_path = PurePosixPath(sel)
                if sel_path.is_absolute():
                    return self.file[sel_path.relative_to('/').as_posix()]
                # If the path is a single name, we can directly access the child
                elif len(sel_path.parts) == 1:
                    return self[sel_path.as_posix()]
                else:
                    grp = sel_path.parts[0]
                    return self[grp][sel_path.relative_to(grp).as_posix()]
            elif sel == '..':
                return self.parent
>           child = self._children[sel]

self = <scippnexus.base.Group object at 0x7fdd810ee300>, sel = 'beam_monitor_m3'

    def __getitem__(self, sel):
        """
        Get a child group or child dataset, a selection of child groups, or load and
        return the current group.
    
        Three cases are supported:
    
        - String name: The child group or child dataset of that name is returned.
        - Class such as ``NXdata`` or ``NXlog``: A dict containing all direct children
          with a matching ``NX_class`` attribute are returned. Also accepts a tuple of
          classes. ``Field`` selects all child fields, i.e., all datasets but not
          groups.
        - Scipp-style index: Load the specified slice of the current group, returning
          a :class:`scipp.DataArray` or :class:`scipp.DataGroup`.
    
        Parameters
        ----------
        sel:
            Child name, class, or index.
    
        Returns
        -------
        :
            Field, group, dict of fields, or loaded data.
        """
        if isinstance(sel, str):
            # We cannot get the child directly from the HDF5 group, since we need to
            # create the parent group, to ensure that fields get the correct properties
            # such as sizes and dtype.
            if '/' in sel:
                sel_path = PurePosixPath(sel)
                if sel_path.is_absolute():
                    return self.file[sel_path.relative_to('/').as_posix()]
                # If the path is a single name, we can directly access the child
                elif len(sel_path.parts) == 1:
                    return self[sel_path.as_posix()]
                else:
                    grp = sel_path.parts[0]
                    return self[grp][sel_path.relative_to(grp).as_posix()]
            elif sel == '..':
                return self.parent
>           child = self._children[sel]
                    ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

    def __getitem__(self, sel):
        """
        Get a child group or child dataset, a selection of child groups, or load and
        return the current group.
    
        Three cases are supported:
    
        - String name: The child group or child dataset of that name is returned.
        - Class such as ``NXdata`` or ``NXlog``: A dict containing all direct children
          with a matching ``NX_class`` attribute are returned. Also accepts a tuple of
          classes. ``Field`` selects all child fields, i.e., all datasets but not
          groups.
        - Scipp-style index: Load the specified slice of the current group, returning
          a :class:`scipp.DataArray` or :class:`scipp.DataGroup`.
    
        Parameters
        ----------
        sel:
            Child name, class, or index.
    
        Returns
        -------
        :
            Field, group, dict of fields, or loaded data.
        """
        if isinstance(sel, str):
            # We cannot get the child directly from the HDF5 group, since we need to
            # create the parent group, to ensure that fields get the correct properties
            # such as sizes and dtype.
            if '/' in sel:
                sel_path = PurePosixPath(sel)
                if sel_path.is_absolute():
                    return self.file[sel_path.relative_to('/').as_posix()]
                # If the path is a single name, we can directly access the child
                elif len(sel_path.parts) == 1:
                    return self[sel_path.as_posix()]
                else:
                    grp = sel_path.parts[0]
                    return self[grp][sel_path.relative_to(grp).as_posix()]
            elif sel == '..':
                return self.parent
>           child = self._children[sel]

self = <scippnexus.base.Group object at 0x7fb3726b1f10>, sel = 'beam_monitor_m3'

    def __getitem__(self, sel):
        """
        Get a child group or child dataset, a selection of child groups, or load and
        return the current group.
    
        Three cases are supported:
    
        - String name: The child group or child dataset of that name is returned.
        - Class such as ``NXdata`` or ``NXlog``: A dict containing all direct children
          with a matching ``NX_class`` attribute are returned. Also accepts a tuple of
          classes. ``Field`` selects all child fields, i.e., all datasets but not
          groups.
        - Scipp-style index: Load the specified slice of the current group, returning
          a :class:`scipp.DataArray` or :class:`scipp.DataGroup`.
    
        Parameters
        ----------
        sel:
            Child name, class, or index.
    
        Returns
        -------
        :
            Field, group, dict of fields, or loaded data.
        """
        if isinstance(sel, str):
            # We cannot get the child directly from the HDF5 group, since we need to
            # create the parent group, to ensure that fields get the correct properties
            # such as sizes and dtype.
            if '/' in sel:
                sel_path = PurePosixPath(sel)
                if sel_path.is_absolute():
                    return self.file[sel_path.relative_to('/').as_posix()]
                # If the path is a single name, we can directly access the child
                elif len(sel_path.parts) == 1:
                    return self[sel_path.as_posix()]
                else:
                    grp = sel_path.parts[0]
                    return self[grp][sel_path.relative_to(grp).as_posix()]
            elif sel == '..':
                return self.parent
>           child = self._children[sel]

self = <scippnexus.base.Group object at 0x7fe8b042e5d0>, sel = 'beam_monitor_m3'

    def __getitem__(self, sel):
        """
        Get a child group or child dataset, a selection of child groups, or load and
        return the current group.
    
        Three cases are supported:
    
        - String name: The child group or child dataset of that name is returned.
        - Class such as ``NXdata`` or ``NXlog``: A dict containing all direct children
          with a matching ``NX_class`` attribute are returned. Also accepts a tuple of
          classes. ``Field`` selects all child fields, i.e., all datasets but not
          groups.
        - Scipp-style index: Load the specified slice of the current group, returning
          a :class:`scipp.DataArray` or :class:`scipp.DataGroup`.
    
        Parameters
        ----------
        sel:
            Child name, class, or index.
    
        Returns
        -------
        :
            Field, group, dict of fields, or loaded data.
        """
        if isinstance(sel, str):
            # We cannot get the child directly from the HDF5 group, since we need to
            # create the parent group, to ensure that fields get the correct properties
            # such as sizes and dtype.
            if '/' in sel:
                sel_path = PurePosixPath(sel)
                if sel_path.is_absolute():
                    return self.file[sel_path.relative_to('/').as_posix()]
                # If the path is a single name, we can directly access the child
                elif len(sel_path.parts) == 1:
                    return self[sel_path.as_posix()]
                else:
                    grp = sel_path.parts[0]
                    return self[grp][sel_path.relative_to(grp).as_posix()]
            elif sel == '..':
                return self.parent
>           child = self._children[sel]
                    ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

    def __getitem__(self, sel):
        """
        Get a child group or child dataset, a selection of child groups, or load and
        return the current group.
    
        Three cases are supported:
    
        - String name: The child group or child dataset of that name is returned.
        - Class such as ``NXdata`` or ``NXlog``: A dict containing all direct children
          with a matching ``NX_class`` attribute are returned. Also accepts a tuple of
          classes. ``Field`` selects all child fields, i.e., all datasets but not
          groups.
        - Scipp-style index: Load the specified slice of the current group, returning
          a :class:`scipp.DataArray` or :class:`scipp.DataGroup`.
    
        Parameters
        ----------
        sel:
            Child name, class, or index.
    
        Returns
        -------
        :
            Field, group, dict of fields, or loaded data.
        """
        if isinstance(sel, str):
            # We cannot get the child directly from the HDF5 group, since we need to
            # create the parent group, to ensure that fields get the correct properties
            # such as sizes and dtype.
            if '/' in sel:
                sel_path = PurePosixPath(sel)
                if sel_path.is_absolute():
                    return self.file[sel_path.relative_to('/').as_posix()]
                # If the path is a single name, we can directly access the child
                elif len(sel_path.parts) == 1:
                    return self[sel_path.as_posix()]
                else:
                    grp = sel_path.parts[0]
                    return self[grp][sel_path.relative_to(grp).as_posix()]
            elif sel == '..':
                return self.parent

self = <scippnexus.base.Group object at 0x7f85c9492570>, sel = 'beam_monitor_m3'

    def __getitem__(self, sel):
        """
        Get a child group or child dataset, a selection of child groups, or load and
        return the current group.
    
        Three cases are supported:
    
        - String name: The child group or child dataset of that name is returned.
        - Class such as ``NXdata`` or ``NXlog``: A dict containing all direct children
          with a matching ``NX_class`` attribute are returned. Also accepts a tuple of
          classes. ``Field`` selects all child fields, i.e., all datasets but not
          groups.
        - Scipp-style index: Load the specified slice of the current group, returning
          a :class:`scipp.DataArray` or :class:`scipp.DataGroup`.
    
        Parameters
        ----------
        sel:
            Child name, class, or index.
    
        Returns
        -------
        :
            Field, group, dict of fields, or loaded data.
        """
        if isinstance(sel, str):
            # We cannot get the child directly from the HDF5 group, since we need to
            # create the parent group, to ensure that fields get the correct properties
            # such as sizes and dtype.
            if '/' in sel:
                sel_path = PurePosixPath(sel)
                if sel_path.is_absolute():
                    return self.file[sel_path.relative_to('/').as_posix()]
                # If the path is a single name, we can directly access the child
                elif len(sel_path.parts) == 1:
                    return self[sel_path.as_posix()]
                else:
                    grp = sel_path.parts[0]
                    return self[grp][sel_path.relative_to(grp).as_posix()]
            elif sel == '..':
                return self.parent
>           child = self._children[sel]
                    ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

    def __getitem__(self, sel):
        """
        Get a child group or child dataset, a selection of child groups, or load and
        return the current group.
    
        Three cases are supported:
    
        - String name: The child group or child dataset of that name is returned.
        - Class such as ``NXdata`` or ``NXlog``: A dict containing all direct children
          with a matching ``NX_class`` attribute are returned. Also accepts a tuple of
          classes. ``Field`` selects all child fields, i.e., all datasets but not
          groups.
        - Scipp-style index: Load the specified slice of the current group, returning
          a :class:`scipp.DataArray` or :class:`scipp.DataGroup`.
    
        Parameters
        ----------
        sel:
            Child name, class, or index.
    
        Returns
        -------
        :
            Field, group, dict of fields, or loaded data.
        """
        if isinstance(sel, str):
            # We cannot get the child directly from the HDF5 group, since we need to
            # create the parent group, to ensure that fields get the correct properties
            # such as sizes and dtype.
            if '/' in sel:
                sel_path = PurePosixPath(sel)
                if sel_path.is_absolute():
                    return self.file[sel_path.relative_to('/').as_posix()]
                # If the path is a single name, we can directly access the child
                elif len(sel_path.parts) == 1:
                    return self[sel_path.as_posix()]
                else:
                    grp = sel_path.parts[0]
                    return self[grp][sel_path.relative_to(grp).as_posix()]
            elif sel == '..':
                return self.parent

self = <scippnexus.base.Group object at 0x7fa5976840e0>, sel = 'beam_monitor_m3'

    def __getitem__(self, sel):
        """
        Get a child group or child dataset, a selection of child groups, or load and
        return the current group.
    
        Three cases are supported:
    
        - String name: The child group or child dataset of that name is returned.
        - Class such as ``NXdata`` or ``NXlog``: A dict containing all direct children
          with a matching ``NX_class`` attribute are returned. Also accepts a tuple of
          classes. ``Field`` selects all child fields, i.e., all datasets but not
          groups.
        - Scipp-style index: Load the specified slice of the current group, returning
          a :class:`scipp.DataArray` or :class:`scipp.DataGroup`.
    
        Parameters
        ----------
        sel:
            Child name, class, or index.
    
        Returns
        -------
        :
            Field, group, dict of fields, or loaded data.
        """
        if isinstance(sel, str):
            # We cannot get the child directly from the HDF5 group, since we need to
            # create the parent group, to ensure that fields get the correct properties
            # such as sizes and dtype.
            if '/' in sel:
                sel_path = PurePosixPath(sel)
                if sel_path.is_absolute():
                    return self.file[sel_path.relative_to('/').as_posix()]
                # If the path is a single name, we can directly access the child
                elif len(sel_path.parts) == 1:
                    return self[sel_path.as_posix()]
                else:
                    grp = sel_path.parts[0]
                    return self[grp][sel_path.relative_to(grp).as_posix()]
            elif sel == '..':
                return self.parent
>           child = self._children[sel]
                    ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

self = <scippnexus.base.Group object at 0x7f9f5254ae70>, sel = 'beam_monitor_m3'

    def __getitem__(self, sel):
        """
        Get a child group or child dataset, a selection of child groups, or load and
        return the current group.
    
        Three cases are supported:
    
        - String name: The child group or child dataset of that name is returned.
        - Class such as ``NXdata`` or ``NXlog``: A dict containing all direct children
          with a matching ``NX_class`` attribute are returned. Also accepts a tuple of
          classes. ``Field`` selects all child fields, i.e., all datasets but not
          groups.
        - Scipp-style index: Load the specified slice of the current group, returning
          a :class:`scipp.DataArray` or :class:`scipp.DataGroup`.
    
        Parameters
        ----------
        sel:
            Child name, class, or index.
    
        Returns
        -------
        :
            Field, group, dict of fields, or loaded data.
        """
        if isinstance(sel, str):
            # We cannot get the child directly from the HDF5 group, since we need to
            # create the parent group, to ensure that fields get the correct properties
            # such as sizes and dtype.
            if '/' in sel:
                sel_path = PurePosixPath(sel)
                if sel_path.is_absolute():
                    return self.file[sel_path.relative_to('/').as_posix()]
                # If the path is a single name, we can directly access the child
                elif len(sel_path.parts) == 1:
                    return self[sel_path.as_posix()]
                else:
                    grp = sel_path.parts[0]
                    return self[grp][sel_path.relative_to(grp).as_posix()]
            elif sel == '..':
                return self.parent
>           child = self._children[sel]
                    ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
